Here's another final implication. One practical implication, finally, the practical implication. Great. It licenses the use of inverse sample covariance matrices of a sufficiently long time series as petition potentially sufficient description of the steady state density. This would furnish a description of the expected flow and Lyapunov exponents to establish whether the system was chaotic or not.
